The cell should also be equipped with surveillance cameras for constant monitoring of SCP-XXXX’s activities. Description: SCP-XXXX is an individual identified as Tommy Oliver, formerly known as the Green Ranger, White Ranger, Red Zeo Ranger, Turbo Red Ranger, and Dino Thunder Black Ranger in various iterations of the Power Rangers media franchise. SCP-XXXX displays a variety of anomalous abilities and attributes, as well as an unparalleled talent for verbal combat and clever comebacks. In addition to its exceptional verbal skills, SCP-XXXX possesses an extensive array of superhuman abilities, including atomic pulse emission, magnetism manipulation, precognition, fireball projection, electric biting, superhuman speed, strength, and senses, laser beam emission, teleportation, powerful jaws, enormous size, fast climbing, gravity beam projection, nuclear/atomic bomb/pulse emission, radioactive heat ray projection, asymmetrically permeable shield, obsidian hardening, and more. SCP-XXXX has been observed demonstrating these abilities both independently and in conjunction with its verbal combat skills. These abilities, when combined with SCP-XXXX’s seemingly limitless endurance, healing factor, and near-invulnerability, make it highly difficult to subdue or contain. SCP-XXXX has shown the ability to withstand attacks from conventional weaponry and SCP objects without any notable effect. SCP-XXXX’s mental manipulation abilities have been observed in situations where it can twist conversations to its advantage, leaving opponents at a loss for words. SCP-XXXX has also been reported to possess an exceptionally high intelligence level, utilizing its keen wit and clever comebacks in verbal altercations, often reducing opponents to silence or some form of psychological defeat. It is currently unknown how SCP-XXXX acquired its abilities or why it possesses such a wide range of anomalous powers. Investigations into SCP-XXXX’s origins and potential weaknesses are ongoing. It is recommended that personnel exercise utmost caution when interacting with SCP-XXXX, as its mental manipulation abilities and physical capabilities pose a significant risk to containment protocols and personnel safety. Addendum: Dr. ██████’s Note: SCP-XXXX’s abilities and attributes seem vastly overpowered and may require revision to appropriately bring the subject under control. Additionally, its clever and witty comebacks have led to several incidents of morale depletion among personnel.
